The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 6 is 0.90. The other faces contribute 4.90 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0.90+4.90)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.97</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>4.90&minus;0.90 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>4.90+0 =</span> <strong>4.9</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.90+4.90 =</span> <strong>5.8</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 3 is 0.50. The other faces contribute 0.75 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0.50+0.75)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.21</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.75&minus;0.50 =</span> <strong>0.25</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.75+0 =</span> <strong>0.75</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.50+0.75 =</span> <strong>1.25</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution from face 5?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(14&minus;12)/12 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.17</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(14&minus;12)<sup>2</sup>/14 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.29</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(14&minus;12)<sup>2</sup>/12 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.33</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(14&minus;12)<sup>2</sup>/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.67</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(14&minus;12)<sup>2</sup> =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 6 is 2.67. The other faces contribute 7.00 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(2.67+7.00)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>1.61</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>7.00&minus;2.67 =</span> <strong>4.33</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>7.00+0 =</span> <strong>7</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>2.67+7.00 =</span> <strong>9.67</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution from face 1?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(7&minus;8)/8 &asymp;</span> <strong>-0.12</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(7&minus;8)<sup>2</sup>/8 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.12</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(7&minus;8)<sup>2</sup>/7 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.14</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(7&minus;8)<sup>2</sup>/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.17</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(7&minus;8)<sup>2</sup> =</span> <strong>1</strong>	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution from face 5?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0&minus;5)/5 =</span> <strong>-1</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0&minus;5)<sup>2</sup>/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>4.17</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0&minus;5)<sup>2</sup>/5 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0&minus;5)<sup>2</sup> =</span> <strong>25</strong>	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution from face 2?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(23&minus;20)/20 =</span> <strong>0.15</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(23&minus;20)<sup>2</sup>/23 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.39</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(23&minus;20)<sup>2</sup>/20 =</span> <strong>0.45</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(23&minus;20)<sup>2</sup>/6 =</span> <strong>1.5</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(23&minus;20)<sup>2</sup> =</span> <strong>9</strong>	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 6 is 0.40. The other faces contribute 0.60 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0.40+0.60)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.17</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.60&minus;0.40 =</span> <strong>0.2</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.60+0 =</span> <strong>0.6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.40+0.60 =</span> <strong>1</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution from face 5?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(10&minus;8)/8 =</span> <strong>0.25</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(10&minus;8)<sup>2</sup>/10 =</span> <strong>0.4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(10&minus;8)<sup>2</sup>/8 =</span> <strong>0.5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(10&minus;8)<sup>2</sup>/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.67</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(10&minus;8)<sup>2</sup> =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution from face 4?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(30&minus;15)/15 =</span> <strong>1</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(30&minus;15)<sup>2</sup>/30 =</span> <strong>7.5</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(30&minus;15)<sup>2</sup>/15 =</span> <strong>15</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(30&minus;15)<sup>2</sup>/6 =</span> <strong>37.5</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(30&minus;15)<sup>2</sup> =</span> <strong>225</strong>	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution from face 4?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(8&minus;10)/10 =</span> <strong>-0.2</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(8&minus;10)<sup>2</sup>/10 =</span> <strong>0.4</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(8&minus;10)<sup>2</sup>/8 =</span> <strong>0.5</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(8&minus;10)<sup>2</sup>/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.67</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(8&minus;10)<sup>2</sup> =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 3 is 0.50. The other faces contribute 0.75 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0.50+0.75)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.21</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.75&minus;0.50 =</span> <strong>0.25</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.75+0 =</span> <strong>0.75</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.50+0.75 =</span> <strong>1.25</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 5 is 1.60. The other faces contribute 4.20 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(1.60+4.20)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.97</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>4.20&minus;1.60 =</span> <strong>2.6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>4.20+0 =</span> <strong>4.2</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>1.60+4.20 =</span> <strong>5.8</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 3 is 15.00. The other faces contribute 15.00 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>15.00&minus;15.00 =</span> <strong>0</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(15.00+15.00)/6 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>15.00+0 =</span> <strong>15</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>15.00+15.00 =</span> <strong>30</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 6 is 0.80. The other faces contribute 10.80 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0.80+10.80)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>1.93</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>10.80&minus;0.80 =</span> <strong>10</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>10.80+0 =</span> <strong>10.8</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.80+10.80 =</span> <strong>11.6</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 2 is 0.80. The other faces contribute 2.10 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0.80+2.10)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.48</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>2.10&minus;0.80 =</span> <strong>1.3</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>2.10+0 =</span> <strong>2.1</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.80+2.10 =</span> <strong>2.9</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 1 is 0.75. The other faces contribute 4.08 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(0.75+4.08)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.81</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>4.08&minus;0.75 =</span> <strong>3.33</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>4.08+0 =</span> <strong>4.08</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>0.75+4.08 =</span> <strong>4.83</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution from face 3?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(17&minus;15)/15 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.13</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(17&minus;15)<sup>2</sup>/17 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.24</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(17&minus;15)<sup>2</sup>/15 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.27</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(17&minus;15)<sup>2</sup>/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.67</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(17&minus;15)<sup>2</sup> =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 3 is 1.07. The other faces contribute 2.80 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(1.07+2.80)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>0.65</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>2.80&minus;1.07 =</span> <strong>1.73</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>2.80+0 =</span> <strong>2.8</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>1.07+2.80 =</span> <strong>3.87</strong>	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Each face contributes (observed &minus; expected)<sup>2</sup> / expected to &chi;<sup>2</sup>. The arithmetic is shown in each choice; no calculator is needed.</p><p>The contribution from face 3 is 1.12. The other faces contribute 6.12 in total. Which calculation combines them to give &chi;<sup>2</sup>?</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>(1.12+6.12)/6 &asymp;</span> <strong>1.21</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6.12&minus;1.12 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6.12+0 =</span> <strong>6.12</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>1.12+6.12 =</span> <strong>7.24</strong>	Correct
